B. Page Brief

{
  "year": 1978,
  "page_type": "department letter",
  "page_slug": "editor_letter",
  "title": "Editor Letter",
  "subtitle": "Letter From the Editor",
  "writer": {
    "name": "BJ Lovestreet, Editor-in-Chief",
    "byline": "By BJ Lovestreet, Editor-in-Chief"
  },
  "source_files": [
    "PROJECT_STATE.md",
    "issues/context/1978_context.json",
    "issues/1978/layout/1978_production_targets.json",
    "issues/1978/layout/1978_issue_map.json",
    "issues/1978/editor_letter.md"
  ],
  "page_purpose": "Present Editor Letter as a complete RetroVerse 1978 department letter page, with page-aware artwork under real typography and no text baked into the illustration layer for `editor_letter`.",
  "story_summary": "Editor Letter Letter From the Editor There is a certain kind of opening sound that can define a year before you even finish the first line of the chorus. In this issue's dataset, \"Stayin' Alive\" by Bee Gees stands at the top with a score of 503, peak position #1, and 23 chart weeks.
